# Crimson Crystal — complete movepool review appendix

**Implemented October 2, 2026:** All 440 reviewed additions and the access fixes are applied. This review is retained as the original audit snapshot; see the [implementation log](C:/Users/luked/Documents/GitHub/Pokemon-Crimson-Crystal/LEARNSET_VARIETY_IMPLEMENTED.md) for current acquisition routes and validation.

_October 2, 2026 · source `a3c945df` · [Main review](C:/Users/luked/Documents/GitHub/Pokemon-Crimson-Crystal/LEARNSET_VARIETY_REVIEW.md)_

P1 = defining role/coverage gap; P2 = worthwhile alternate build; P3 = already healthy, optional refinement; Keep = intentional/restricted identity or already sufficient. The rank is a design priority, not a tier ranking. Modern references below use Showdown source codes: 9/8 = generation; M = machine, T = tutor, E = egg, L[number] = level-up. BDSP sources are labeled separately. G4–7 entries are older options, not asserted to be available in Scarlet/Violet. A source may belong to an official pre-evolution; the JSON includes its exact origins and the source catalog for every code.

Current role tags summarize accessible tools, not independently proven viable sets. For example, widespread Curse/Work Up can produce a physical-setup tag on a special attacker, and a low-stat special boost is still mechanically present. The individual explanations and four-move examples make the actual build judgment.

Other developing stages were included in the source/pool audit and should receive family additions selectively according to their type, offensive stats and evolution pacing. Babies and cocoons need coherent progression; they do not need the full breadth or strongest attacks of their final evolutions.

## Complete family egg-list review (213 mapped roots)

Every mapped family is listed here, including empty or nonbreeding lists. Current choices are the actual list used by the family tutor. “Alternate access” means the move is already obtainable without the egg list on every terminal outcome in that family; it may still be valuable for early access and is not automatically a removal recommendation. The terminal reviews above assess whether the available choices fit the actual final typing, stats and abilities.

Breeding notes describe structural eligibility from the ROM’s gender/egg-group data. They do not certify a natural donor chain, capture availability or four-move combination. The family tutor directly supplies listed moves. A proposal shown here remains specific to the named form: use a form-specific learnset/tutor for branch-restricted moves. Only additions with an E source in the per-choice evidence have explicit modern egg precedent; modern machine/tutor access alone is not egg precedent.
